2026-11-09T07:46:31.1556033Z SOURCE_SHA="$GITHUB_SHA"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1556386Z SHORT_SHA="${SOURCE_SHA::7}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1556692Z PUBLISH_MODE="none"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1556943Z PR_IMAGE_TAG=""
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1557178Z PUSH_OUTPUT="false"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1557429Z 
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1557757Z # workflow_call path (called from integration.yml). The planner only
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1558265Z # invokes us when a build is actually needed (no pin/cache match), so
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1558742Z # we always publish. The publish mode follows the caller's event:
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1559232Z # pull_request -> pr-scoped image (consumed by the calling Integration
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1559722Z # run via digest output, not by tag polling); main/schedule/dispatch
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1560242Z # -> pin-refresh image (operator opens a reviewed image-pin.json PR
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1560622Z # afterwards).
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1560901Z if [[ -n "$EXPECTED_FINGERPRINT" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1561209Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1561607Z   if [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "pull_request" && "$PR_HEAD_REPO" == "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1562246Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pr"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1562575Z     PR_IMAGE_TAG="${IMAGE_NAME}:pr-${PR_NUMBER}-${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1563020Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1563391Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1563773Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" || "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1564177Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1564410Z   else
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1564718Z     # Untrusted PR (fork) reaching workflow_call should have been
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1565137Z     # blocked at the planner; defend in depth here too.
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1566032Z     echo "::error::workflow_call invoked from unsupported event ($EVENT_NAME, head_repo=$PR_HEAD_REPO)." >&2
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1566542Z     exit 1
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1566754Z   fi
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1567084Z # Standalone path: push to main, weekly schedule, or manual dispatch.
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1567576Z # These refresh the reviewed image-pin.json digest. The pull_request
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1568043Z # trigger no longer exists on this workflow; PR builds reach us only
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1568446Z # through the workflow_call branch above.
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1568837Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1569207Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1569456Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1569731Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1570032Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1570273Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1570565Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1570914Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="${DISPATCH_PUSH}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1571231Z   if [[ "$DISPATCH_PUSH" == "true" ]]; then
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1571525Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1571769Z   fi
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1571977Z fi
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1572176Z {
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1572386Z   echo "source_sha=${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1572681Z   echo "short_sha=${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1573003Z   echo "cache_key=ijt-browser-ci-${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1573323Z   echo "push=${PUSH_OUTPUT}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1573891Z   echo "publish_mode=${PUBLISH_MODE}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1574206Z   echo "pr_image_tag=${PR_IMAGE_TAG}"
2026-11-09T07:46:31.1574495Z } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
